H&amp;R Block, is the world's largest Tax Service provider company with over 800 Million returns filed worldwide annually. We came to India in 2012 with an aim to provide easy tax filing solutions to millions of individuals across the country. H&amp;R Block provides services of Individual Income Tax Filing, U.S. Expat Tax Filing, Business Tax preparation and GST managed services.<br/><br/>

Individual tax Preparation services are offered through two channels – <a href="https://www.hrblock.in/online-income-tax-e-filing-experts?utm_source=homepage&amp;utm_medium=fat_foo&amp;utm_campaign=fy_19_20" rel="noopener" style="color: #7dc242;" target="_blank">Income Tax e-Filing by Experts</a>, and Self e-Filing of Income Tax Returns. Income tax Filing by Experts is a service where a dedicated tax expert is assigned to you, to help you understand your tax modalities, prepare returns for you and file your ITR online. If you prefer doing your taxes yourself, H&amp;R Block provides a highly intuitive online tool for <a href="https://www.hrblock.in/e-filing-income-tax-returns?utm_source=homepage&amp;utm_medium=fat_foo&amp;utm_campaign=fy_19_20" rel="noopener" style="color: #7dc242;" target="_blank">e-Filing income tax returns</a> which automatically extracts all data from your Form 16, to help you file your taxes online yourself in 3 easy steps.<br/><br/>
<a href="https://www.hrblock.in/business-income-tax-return-filing?utm_source=homepage&amp;utm_medium=fat_foo&amp;utm_campaign=fy_19_20" rel="noopener" style="color: #7dc242;" target="_blank">Business tax preparation services</a> are provided through personal interaction as well as online for filing ITR 3 and ITR 4. Our <a href="https://www.hrblock.in/gst/?utm_source=homepage&amp;utm_medium=fat_foo&amp;utm_campaign=fy_19_20" rel="noopener" style="color: #7dc242;" target="_blank">GST managed services</a> are a complete solution for all the GST compliance worries where we take care of everything for you right from registration, reconciliation as well as return filing. Apart from this we also provide tax tools like Income Tax Calculator, Advance tax calculator and a downloadable Income tax ready Reckoner for quick reference.<br/><br/>

Our customers choose us over other service providers, as we offer a blend of tax filing methods suited to the needs of our clients as well as dedicated year-round support. With more than 500 corporate partnerships and over 6 Lakh clients, we are the ‘Largest Tax Services provider for Individuals and Businesses’ in India with a strong focus on client satisfaction.
